## Document 025: 2026-10-30_［QE予測］7～9月期実質GDPは年率＋1.0％とプラス成長を予想（1次QE）_4f5741cf

7～9 月期実質GDP は年率＋1.0％と 
プラス成長を予想（1 次QE）

○ 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比＋0.2％（年率＋1.0％）と、4～6月期（前期比＋
0.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半期連続でのプラス成長を予想 
○ 個人消費が伸び悩む一方、設備投資や民間在庫が押し上げる見込み。なお、公的在庫と輸入では
中東情勢を主因とする一時的な変動が生じ、成長率にプラス・マイナス両面で影響 
○ 年度後半の日本経済は、小幅なプラス成長を予想。インフレ再加速を受け消費抑制が続く一方、設
備投資が堅調に拡大。日本銀行は12月会合で政策金利を1.50％に引き上げる見込み

7～9月期の実質GDP（1次
速報値）はプラス成長 を
予想 
11月16日 （月） に、 内閣府から2026年7～9月期の 「四半期別GDP速 報（QE）」
（1次速報値）が公表される。現時点で入手可能な基礎統計の結果を踏まえ
ると、7～9月期の実質GDPは前期比＋0.2％（ 年 率＋1.0％） となる見込みだ。
4～6月期の前期比＋0.4％（年率＋1.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半
期連続でプラス成長になると予想する（図表）。 
7～9月期は、内需の柱である個人消費が伸び悩む一方、 設備投資の拡大や、
製造業を中心とする在庫積み増しが成長率を押し上げるとみられる。その他
では、前期の備蓄原油放出の反動が公的在庫投資のプラス要因として現れる
が、原油の代替調達進展を反映した輸入の増加がマイナス要因になるなど、 
図表 2026 年7～9 月期GDP（1 次速報）予測

前期比に対する寄与度（％Pt）

### Page 2

2

中東情勢による一時的な変動も引き続き成長率に影響する見込みだ。以下、
需要項目別に7～9月期の経済動向を詳しく確認しよう。 
個人消費は伸び悩み 。耐
久財の特需剥落や、 根強
い節約志向が影響 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％（4～6月期：同＋0.0％） と、2四半期連続で伸び
悩む結果になろう。消費の弱さの背景には、 複合的な要因があると考えられ
る。 
耐久財では、自動車とエアコンの「特需」剥落が押し下げ要因になろう。
自動車は、購入時にかかる自動車税の環境性能割が3月末に廃止されたこと
を受け、4月から自動車販売が過去平均に比べ上振れていたが、8月以降は再
び過去平均並みの水準に戻っている。エアコンは、2027年4月の省エネ基準
引き上げで販売価格が上昇するとの見方から駆け込み需要が生じていたが、
景気ウォッチャー調査のコメントなどをみると、8月に入った後は一服した
模様だ。9月は気温が低かったほか、長雨による客足の減少もエアコンなど
季節家電の売り上げに悪影響を及ぼしたとみられる。 
食品やエネルギー、 各種消耗品などの非耐久財では、物価高を背景とする
根強い節約志向により、 弱含みが続いているようだ。今回のインフレ局面が
本格化する前の2020年と比較すると、非耐久消費財の物価は足元までの約6
年半でおよそ1.3倍の水準に上昇した計算になる。こうした累積的な価格上
昇が、 消費者の体感物価の高まりを通じて節約志向を強め、 購入数量の抑制
につながっている可能性がある。 
サービス消費は、 イベント関連支出などで底堅さがみられる一方、日常的
な支出には物価高による節約志向が波及し、 サービスや価格帯、 顧客層によ
る二極化が進みつつあるとみられる。 また、8～9月には台風・長雨による旅
行・ 宿泊のキャンセルや外出控えの影響から、 レジャー関連を中心に消費が
伸び悩んだようだ。 
設備投 資は3四半期ぶり
に前期比プラス 
設備投資は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.9％） と、3四半期ぶりに前期
比プラスへ転じると予想する。 
直近の設備投資関連指標をみると、資本財の国内向け供給量を表す資本財
総供給指数（除く輸送機械）が7～8月に大きく上昇した。また第3次産業活
動指数では、ソフトウェア業の実質売上高が同じく7～8月に増加したことが
確認でき、どちらも7～9月期の設備投資が拡大したことを示唆している。 
加えて、前期の4～6月期に発生した一時的な押し下げ圧力（特許権の海外
譲渡による国内研究開発投資の減少） がなくなったこともプラス要因だ。た
だし、 これは裏を返せば、7～9月期のQEにおける設備投資の伸びが実態以上
に強い数字になるということでもあり、評価する際には留意が必要である。 
住宅投資は 前期比マイナ
スが継続 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.8％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.6％ ）と、2四半期連続で前期
比マイナスとなろう。中東情勢を背景とする資材不足は徐々に緩和している
とみられるが、7～8月の住宅着工件数が伸び悩んでおり、住宅価格や金利上

### Page 3

3

昇による需要低迷に加えて、 足元では地震や台風などによる着工 ・ 進捗の遅
れが発生している可能性がある。 
民間 在庫は 2四半期連続
で成長率にプラス寄与 
民間在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は、＋0.2％Pt（4～6月期：
＋0.3％Pt）とプラスが続く見込みだ。AI関連需要の強さなどを背景に、製
造業で在庫積み増しの動きが進んでいることが影響すると考えられる。な
お、4～6月期は石油化学製品などの供給不足を懸念した前倒し発注が在庫を
押し上げたとみられるが、7～9月期は原油の代替調達進展に伴って供給不足
が緩和したことで、そうした要因の影響は一巡した模様だ。 
公的需要 は備蓄原油放出
の反動で前期比プラスに 
公的需要は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.8％）と、2四半期ぶりの前期
比プラスを予想する。 
最大の要因は、公的在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度がプラスに
転じるとみられることだ。4～6月期は国家備蓄原油の放出により公的在庫変
動の寄与度が▲0.5％Ptと大幅マイナスになったが、7～9月期は国家原油備
蓄の減少が小幅なものにとどまり（6月末：105日分→9月末：102日分）、公
的在庫の減少ペースが緩和することで、成長率に対し＋0.3％Pt程度のプラ
ス寄与になると見込まれる。 
その他の公的需要項目では、政府消費を前期比＋0.2％（4～6月期：同＋
1.7％）と予想する。4～6月期は高校授業料・小学校給食費の無償化開始に
より高い伸びになったが、7～9月期はそうした特殊要因が剥落し、緩やかな
増加ペースに戻ると考えられる。公共投資は前期比▲0.5％（4～6月期：同
▲0.5％）と、土木関連工事の減少を受けて2四半期連続のマイナスを見込む。 
輸出が小幅に減少する 一
方、 輸入が増加し、外需は
4四半期ぶりのマイナス
寄与 
外需では、輸出が前期比▲0.3％（4～6月期：同＋0.4％）と小幅に減少す
る一方、輸 入が同＋1.3％（4～6月 期：同▲1.7％）と増加に転じる見込みだ。
実質GDP成長率に対する外需の寄与度は▲0.3％Ptと、4四半期ぶりのマイナ
スが予想される。 
輸入の増加は、原油の代替調達進展が主因だ。中東情勢の緊迫化により4
～6月期は一時的に原油輸入が急減したが、7～9月期は米国産原油やホルム
ズ海峡を通過しない中東産原油の調達が増え、9月を除いて前年並みの輸入
量が確保されている。 
輸入はGDPの控除項目であることから、7～9月期は輸入増加が成長率の下
押し要因になる。 ただし、代替調達の進展に伴って国家備蓄原油の放出が抑
制され、 先述したように7～9月期は公的在庫変動が成長率にプラス寄与する
とみられることから、輸入増による成長率押し下げ効果の一部は公的在庫変
動により相殺されるだろう。 
輸出では、前期の4～6月期に特許権の海外譲渡がサービス輸出を押し上げ
た （これは4～6月期の設備投資の押し下げ要因でもある） 反動が現れ、 一時
的に下振れするだろう。一方、旺盛なAI関連需要が支えになり、半導体・電

### Page 4

4

子部品や半導体製造装置などの財輸出は堅調に推移する見込みだ。インバウ
ンド需要 （非居住者家計の国内での直接購入） は、 日中対立の継続による中
国人訪日客の少なさに加え、 航空運賃上昇を受けた欧米訪日客の伸び悩みに
より、低調な結果になると見込まれる。 
2026 年度後半 の実質 GDP
は小幅プラス と予想 。個
人消費が伸び悩む一方、
設備投資が堅調拡大 
以上のとおり、7～9月期の日本経済は前期から減速するものの、4四半期
連続でプラス成長になると予想する。GDPの約半分を占める個人消費の伸び
悩みが足を引っ張っているが、設備投資や製造業を中心とする在庫積み増し
がけん引する格好だ。 
先行きについてみると、2026年度後半（2026年10～2027年3月）の 実質GDP
は、 均してみれば小幅なプラス成長になると予想する。個人消費が振るわな
い一方で、 設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が続くだろう。2026年度全体の
実質GDP成長率は、前年度末の堅調な伸びで今年度の平均水準が押し上げら
れることもあり（いわゆるプラスのゲタ）、前年比＋1％程度とまずまずの
成長率になる計算だ。 
年度後半の個人消費では、インフレ再加速による実質賃金の伸び悩みが消
費拡大の抑制要因になるだろう。 原油 ・ ナフサ価格高騰の影響が徐々に波及
し、 コア消費者物価指数 （生鮮食品を除く総合） は年度末にかけて前年比＋
3％程度まで上昇すると予想される（ただし、2026年末から政府の電気・ガ
ス料金支援策が実施されれば、0.4～0.5％Pt程度の押し下げ要因になる可能
性がある）。加えて、2027年4月の消費税減税開始前には、保存食品などを
中心とした買い控えの動きが生じるとみられ、 一時的に消費が落ち込む可能
性がある。 
他方で、年度後半の設備投資は堅調に拡大するだろう。日銀短観（9月調
査） では、2026年度の設備投資計画 （ソフトウェア・研究開発を含む設備投
資額 （除く土地投資額）） が前年比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持した （6月 調 査：
同＋8.8％）。生産・営業用設備投資判断DIが2025年度後半以降に低下し、
設備の不足感が強まっていることをあわせて考えれば、 設備投資は今後拡大
傾向で推移していく見込みだ。 
また、政府の投資支援策である「大胆な投資促進税制」 （特定生産性向上
設備等投資促進税制）が2026年7月末から開始されたことも、今後の設備投
資にとってプラス要因になろう。同税制における減税規模の政府見込みを参
考に試算すると、2026～2028年度に設備投資の前年比変化率を約0.1％Ptず
つ押し上げる効果があると考えられる。 
ただし、 日銀が来年にかけて政策金利を一段と引き上げ、 企業の新規借入
金利がさらに上昇すると予想されること、 また、 投資財の価格が上昇傾向を
続けると考えられることを踏まえると、 中小企業を中心に設備投資を控える
企業が今後増加する可能性がある点には注意が必要だ。

### Page 5

5

日銀は10月会合で 政策金
利を据え置き 。次回利上
げは12月と予想 
10月29・30日に開催された金融政策決定会合において、 日本銀行は政策金
利を現行の1.25％で据え置いた。次の利上げ（1.25％→1.50％） は、次回12
月17・18日の会合と予想する。 前回利上げを行った9月会合に続いて 「3カ月
に1回」ペースでの利上げになり、 日銀の利上げペースが6月以前の 「半年強
に1回」から加速していることを印象付けるものになろう。 
企業の価格転嫁行動は一段と積極化しており、植田総裁が9月会合後の記
者会見で 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明したように、日本銀行は「物価引
き上げ」 から 「物価上振れ抑制」 へと金融政策運営の舵を切っている。12月
の追加利上げ後も、 金利上昇の影響を確認しながら、 日銀は最終的に2027年
半ばにかけて政策金利を2.0％まで引き上げていくだろう。 
なお、経済見通しの詳細については、みずほ総合研究所調査部「2026年・
2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ─ 転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方
を探る ─」（10月2日）をご覧いただきたい。 
（https://www.mizuhobank.co.jp/corporate/mhri/research/report/pdf/outlook261002.pdf）

## Document 075: 2026年9月雇用統計

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月雇用統計.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

日本

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9番 1号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026年 10月 30日 全8頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 9月雇用統計 
失業率は2.4％と前月から0.1％pt低下し、就業拡大が進展

経済調査部 エコノミスト 菊池 慈陽

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 9月の完全失業率（季節調整値）は 2.4％と、2カ月ぶりに低下（前月差▲
0.1％pt）し、7月の水準に戻った。失業者数は「自発的な離職」を中心に 2カ月ぶり
に減少（同▲10万人）し、就業者数は2カ月連続で増加（同＋34万人）した。労働参
加と就業拡大が進展し、雇用環境は前月から改善したとみられる。 
◼ 2026年9月の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は1.19倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と3カ月ぶり
に上昇した。新規求職申込件数が大きく減少したことで、新規求人倍率は2.19倍（同
＋0.13pt）と上昇した。 
◼ 先行きの雇用環境は堅調に推移しよう。労働供給が中長期的に減少していく可能性が
高いこともあり、企業は高水準の賃上げなど、人材確保に向けた積極的な取り組みを
続けている。ただし、雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない。中東情勢をめぐる不確実
性は依然として高く、長期化する原油高が企業収益を悪化させ、幅広い産業で労働需
要を下押しする恐れがある。

9月の完全失業率：2.4％と前月から低下、「自発的な離職」による失業者が大きく減少 
2026年 9月の完全失業率（季節調整値）は2.4％と、2カ月ぶりに低下（前月差▲0.1％pt）
し、7月の水準に戻った（図表2左上） 。失業者数は2カ月ぶりに減少（同▲10万人）し、就業
者数は 2カ月連続で増加（同＋34万人）した。労働参加と就業拡大が進展し、雇用環境は前月
から改善したとみられる。 
失業者数を求職理由別に見ると、「自発的な離職」 （前月差▲13万人）が大きく減少し、全体
を押し下げた。「勤め先や事業の都合による離職」（同▲1万人）は小幅に減少した。他方、「定
年又は雇用契約の満了」（同＋1万人）と「新たに求職」（同＋1万人）は小幅に増加した（巻末
の雇用概況①下段左）。 
就業者数の前月からの変化を業種別に見ると、「製造業」と「医療，福祉」が大きく増加し、
全体を押し上げた。「卸売業，小売業」と「情報通信業」も増加した。他方、「建設業」やその
他の非製造業は減少した。（図表2左下）。 
雇用者数（役員を除く）を雇用形態別に見ると、正規雇用者は前月から 22万人増加した。
2026年春頃から増加傾向にあり、ピークだった2025年 11月の水準を上回った。非正規雇用者
は 5カ月ぶりに増加（前月差＋21万人）した（図表 2右下）。

9月の新規求人倍率：求職が大きく減少した一方で求人は増加し、前月から上昇 
2026年 9月の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は 1.19倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と 3カ月ぶりに上
昇した。新規求人倍率は2.19倍（同＋0.13pt）と上昇した（図表3左）。 
求人側の動きを見ると、有効求人数は前月から横ばいだった一方、新規求人数（前月比＋
1.5％）は 2カ月ぶりに増加した（図表 4左） 。求職側では、有効求職者数（同▲1.4％）、新規
求職申込件数（同▲4.4％）はいずれも 3カ月ぶりに減少した。求職側は 2025年春頃から持ち
直しの動きが見られており、求人側も2026年春頃から増加傾向にある。 
雇用形態別に見ると、正社員の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は1.01倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と
3カ月ぶりに上昇した。新規求人倍率も 1.79倍（同＋0.11pt）と 3カ月ぶりに上昇した（図表
3右）。

先行き：堅調な推移を見込むも、中東情勢など雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない 
先行きの雇用環境は、堅調に推移することを見込んでいる。労働供給が中長期的に減少して
いく可能性が高いこともあり、企業は高水準の賃上げなど1、人材確保に向けた積極的な取り組
みを続けている。 
日本銀行「全国企業短期経済観測調査」 （日銀短観）の2026年 9月調査では、雇用人員判断
DI（全規模全産業、 「過剰」－「不足」 ）は「最近」が▲38％ptと 6月調査から 1％pt低下し
（人手不足感が強まり）、「先行き」は▲41％ptと更なる低下が予想されている（巻末の雇用概
況①上段右）。特に、中小企業（全産業）は「最近」が▲42％ptと6月調査から3％pt低下し、
「先行き」は▲45％ptだった。中小企業を中心に、人手不足感が強まっているとみられる。 
株式会社ナウキャストによる「HRog賃金 Now」の求人数指数を見ると、正社員とパート・ア
ルバイトのいずれも 2026年春頃から上昇傾向にある（巻末の雇用概況③下段左）。人手不足感
の強まりを受けて、今後も労働需要は堅調に推移するとみられる。 
他方、トランプ米政権による高関税政策（トランプ関税）1F
2や、長期化する日中関係の冷え込
みは、引き続き労働需要の下押し要因となるだろう。 
また、雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない。中東情勢は依然として不確実性が高い状況が続
いており、とりわけ注意が必要だ。原油等の供給網の正常化は当面見込みにくく、輸入原油価
格が高止まりしている。原油高は原材料費や物流費などの上昇を通じて企業収益を悪化させ3、
幅広い産業で労働需要を下押しする可能性がある。

1 日本労働組合総連合会（連合）が公表した第 7回（最終）回答集計結果では、定期昇給相当込みの賃上げ率
（加重平均）が企業規模計で 5.01％と、前年（5.25％）をやや下回るものの、高水準を維持している。300人
未満の中小組合では 4.69％と前年（4.65％）をわずかながら上回り、中小企業の積極的な姿勢が目立った。詳
細は、日本労働組合総連合会（連合）「3年連続の 5％超え！～2026春季生活闘争 第7回（最終）回答集計結
果について～」（2026年7月3日）を参照。 
2 トランプ米政権は通商法 122条に基づく措置が失効する 7月 24日に新たな関税措置を発動した（日本経済新
聞 電子版「トランプ政権が新関税発動、日本 12.5％ 中間選挙へ看板政策継続」（2026年7月 24日））。 
3 原油高による物価の上昇については、神田慶司・畑中宏仁・中村華奈子・横田凱「日本経済見通し：2026年
8月」（大和総研レポート、2026年8月 24日）を参照。

華邦電 (2344 TT)  Trading Buy

記憶體報價漲幅收斂，建議 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ： 4Q26 市場需求呈現結構性分化。 PC 及傳統消費性
電子進入季節性淡季與庫存修正，需求鬆軟； AI 相關產品如 AI
伺服器、 Edge AI 及網基礎建設的需求力道維持強勁，延續至
2027 年，記憶體產業仍呈現供需吃緊，預估華邦電 2026 年和
2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 23.68 元和 35.90 元。除 AI 伺服器需求
外，交換器及 Edge AI 應用亦帶動 NOR Flash 與 SLC NAND 需
求持續成長，華邦電憑藉 NOR Flash 全球市占率第一及 SLC 
NAND 擴產，可望持續受惠。此外，矽電容目前滿載，新客戶
陸續加入，將成為公司新的獲利來源。雖 2027 年記憶體報價維
持高檔，但消費性電子需求較差，記憶體報價漲幅收斂，影響
華邦電部份業績，因此建議為 Trading Buy ，目標價 162 元。  
華邦電 3Q26 稅後 EPS 7.87 元：記憶體報價上漲， 3Q26 華邦電
合併營收 823.31 億元， QoQ+37.6%(YoY+278.2%) 。CMS 占營收
比重 58% 、 Flash 占營收比重 32% 、 Logic 占 9%，其中 CMS 營
收 QoQ+50% ，成長最明顯。 3Q26 華邦電記憶體營收為 742.84
億元， QoQ+43.7% ， YoY+425.6% 。 Flash 和 CMS 各占記憶體營
收 36% 和 64% 。 3Q26 合併毛利率為  70.57% ，增加約  4.32 個
百分點。 3Q26 記憶體的毛利率從  2Q26 的  70.3% 提升至  
73.9% ， 3Q26 稅後 EPS 7.87 元。  
資本支出 395 億元 ：預估 2026 年資本支出為 395 億元，
YoY+618.18% 。部分原訂於 2026 年底交貨的設備將遞延跨年至
2027 年付款，但整體擴產進度超前，來自製程的轉換。目前台
中廠  DRAM 月產能約 12K 。配合高雄廠  16nm 新產能於 2027
年開出，公司規劃於  2027 年將台中廠  DRAM 月產能降至約
7K。高雄廠  Module A 目前月產能約 15K，預計在 2026 年底前
提升至 24K 並開始新增產能投片。 16nm 月產能目前約 2K。華
邦電已正式啟動高雄廠  Module B 的全新階段擴產計畫，目標
2029 年年中開始裝機，並於  2029 年底前進入量產。

華邦電 3Q26 營收 823.31 億元， QoQ+37.58% ，稅後淨利 354.22 億元，
QoQ+45.67% ，稅後 EPS 7.87 元：  
受惠記憶體報價上漲， 3Q26 華邦電合併營收 823.31 億元，
QoQ+37.6%(YoY+278.2%) 。 CMS( 原 Specialty DRAM 改名客製化記憶體方
案 )占營收比重 58% 、 Flash 占營收比重 32% 、 Logic 占 9%，其中 CMS 營收
QoQ+50% ，成長最明顯。 3Q26 合併毛利率為 70.57% ，增加約 4.32 個百分
點。 3Q26 華邦電記憶體營收為 742.84 億元， QoQ+43.7% ， YoY+425.6% 。
Flash 和 CMS 各占記憶體營收 36% 和 64% 。華邦電 3Q26 合併毛利率為
70.6% ，較 2Q26 的 66.2% 增加約 4 個百分點。 3Q26 記憶體的毛利率從 2Q26
的 70.3% 提升至 73.9% ，產能利用率維持約 100% 的滿載狀態，邏輯 (新唐 )毛

3 群益投顧 
利率則為 37.4% 。  
營業費用方面， 3Q26 營業費用為 143.17 億元 (費用率 18%) ，合併營益率為
53.2% ，歸屬母公司業主淨利為 354.22 億元， QoQ+45.67% ，稅後 EPS 7.87
元。每股淨值從 2Q26 的 36.67 元增加至 46.04 元。  
若單獨以記憶體來看， 3Q26 記憶體產品營收比重方面， Flash 產品線占記憶
體營收比重 36% ，營收 QoQ+34.0% 、 YoY+255% ，位元出貨量 (bit 
shipment)QoQ 持平、 YoY 增加 11~13% ，在 NOR 與 SLC NAND 供給偏緊的
態勢下，推升 ASP(US$)QoQ 增加 31~33% 、 YoY 增加近 200% 。 32nm Flash
占 Flash 營收比重為 17%，24nm 占比達 7%，製程持續轉進； 46nm 占 16%、
45nm Flash 占比為 3%。高容量產品線整體出貨占比持續提升，高容量 NOR 
Flash 營收與出貨占比均創新高，反映 NOR 與 SLC NAND 需求暢旺， 24nm
營收貢獻擴增，受惠於製程技術持續轉換及產品組合優化。  
CMS 占記憶體營收 64%，營收 QoQ+50% 、YoY+620% ，位元出貨量 QoQ 減
少 7~9% 、 YoY 增加 4~6% ，資料中心、 AI 伺服器、 Edge AI 及車用等應用
帶動結構性供給缺口 ， 使得ASP(US$)QoQ 增加近 60%、YoY 增加超過 500%。
3Q26 的 20nm 占 CMS 營收比重達 66%，仍為主要成長動能，主要受惠於 AI
強勁需求，反映 DDR4/LPDDR4 產品線的強勁銷售表現， 16nm 開始放量並
貢獻 3%營收，預期自 2027 年起帶來顯著營收貢獻。  
從產品應用比重來看，通訊電子 (Communication) 占記憶體營收比重 31% ，
QoQ+55% ，受惠於網通等應用需求；車用及工業用相關 (Car & Industrial) 占
營收比重 26% ， QoQ+36% ；消費性電子 (Consumer) 占營收比重 26% ，
QoQ+35% ；電腦相關 (Computer) 占 17%， QoQ+66% 。相關 AI 伺服器、 Edge 
AI、車用與基礎架構應用對高容量記憶體與 Code Storage Flash 的需求持續
強勁。

全年的記憶體資本支出約新台幣 395 億元， YoY+618.18% ，其中生產設備資

4 群益投顧 
本支出約占 95%( 特別是晶圓廠設備 )，以加速增產。儘管目前許多機器設備
交期拉長，部分原訂於 2026 年底交貨的設備將遞延跨年至 2027 年付款，但
整體擴產進度不僅如期，甚至超前，來自製程的轉換。 1Q26~3Q26 已實際支
出新台幣 234 億元。  
目前台中廠 DRAM 月產能約 12K 。配合高雄廠 16nm 新產能於 2027 年開
出，公司規劃於 2027 年將台中廠 DRAM 月產能降至約 7K。高雄廠 Module 
A 目前月產能約 15K ，預計在 2026 年底前提升至 24K 並開始新增產能投
片。 16nm 月產能目前約 2K，現階段良率接近 80% ，目標在 2026 年底前提
升至 90% 以上。長期理想目標是將 16nm 產能拉高至 16K ，逐步取代舊製
程。受惠於 16nm 產能的開出，預估 2027 年高雄廠的位元產出將接近倍增。
高雄廠 Module A 達到 24K 產能後，至 2028 年底前空間已塞滿，因此華邦
電已正式啟動高雄廠 Module B 的全新階段擴產計畫，目標在 2026 年底正
式動工，預計 2029 年年中開始裝機，並於 2029 年底前進入量產。 Module B
的潔淨室面積約 30,000 平方公尺，約為現有 Module A 的兩倍。完整裝滿後
可容納約 50K~60K 的月產能 ， 但公司強調會配合客戶的預測與 LTA( 長期合
約 )採分階段裝機，不會一次裝滿。規劃生產 Standard DRAM 、 CUBE 
DRAM( 客製化記憶體 )、 Wafer-on-Wafer( 晶圓堆疊 )及矽電容 (Si-Cap) 。
Module B 已預留 14nm 、12nm 及 EUV( 極紫外光 )的製程空間。其中 14nm 將
同時開發非 EUV 及 EUV 版本， EUV 版為後續 12nm 做準備。因 EUV 設備
交期長達 3 至 3.5 年，公司必須提前向設備廠規劃。已有客戶開始提前洽談
2029 至 2030 年 Module B 的產能與 LTA 。  
公司先前投入約新台幣 40 億元建立額外的矽電容產線，目前產能已全數售
罄 。 矽電容採用獨立產線配置 ， 因此擴產與裝機完全不會排擠到 16nm DRAM
的產能與設備規劃。由於產能供不應求，公司目前正積極在高雄廠尋找新的
空間以利後續擴產。此外， 11/2026 華邦電董事會通過 730 億資本支出，主
要用於高雄廠新廠 Module B 的建置與裝機費用。  
圖一：華邦電記憶體資本支出

資料來源：華邦電，群益投顧預估整理 
華邦電在矽電容取得 MLCC 廠和 AI 晶片的客戶：  
華邦電的矽電容起初並非獨立規劃的生意，而是華邦電在開發客製化記憶
體 (CUBE) 時，為解決電壓驟降 (voltage drop) 而提供給客戶的保護電路。由

### Page 5

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 4 日

5 群益投顧 
於產品規格極佳，部分非客製化記憶體的客戶進而主動要求試作，華邦電順
勢將其發展成一項獨立產品。  
隨著 AI、先進封裝 (如 CoWoS 等 )及細微穩壓 (1.2 伏、 0.9 伏以下 )的需求爆
發，矽電容因具備極小、極薄、耐高溫且電容值精準的特性，迅速成為解決
封裝雜訊與穩壓的最佳方案。  
台積電雖為全球最大的矽電容製造商，但其矽電容多整合於自身先進封裝
(如  CoWoS 中介層 )內搭售，不對外單獨銷售。華邦電採取純晶圓代工模式，
提供  PDK ，讓客戶或 Design House 自行設計，由華邦電負責生產與專屬機
台投片。客戶群涵蓋 MLCC 大廠與 AI 晶片廠，應用於光通訊模組、 RF 射
頻模組與  3D 封裝。  
為避免矽電容生產擠壓到利潤更好的 DRAM 產能，華邦電特別為矽電容開
闢獨立的生產專線。公司先前已針對此專線投資約新台幣 40 億元，華邦電
在高雄廠 Module A 設置矽電容專用產線，現有產能約數千片晶圓 /月，前期
開出產能已全數售罄。面對新增客戶少量需求 (如 500 片至 2,000 片 )，目前
採產能彈性調配方式供應。  
技術規格方面，華邦電矽電容的容值達到 5,400nF/mm²(5.4µF/mm²) ，高於同
業之 1,500nF/mm² 或 2,700nF/mm² 。產品厚度約 10µm ，適配於 PCB 埋入、
CoWoS 載板 (Substrate) 設置或晶片堆疊架構，應用涵蓋 AI 加速器、 3D 封
裝、光模組及高頻 RF 領域。全球前幾大 MLCC 廠商透過華邦電晶圓代工
(Foundry) 模式與 PDK 進行設計及投片。  
未來產能擴充規劃方面，高雄廠 Module B 已預留矽電容專用機台與專線空
間，後續將依長期合約 (LTA) 進度開出產能。矽電容製程僅使用 DRAM 前段
約五分之一設備 (製造電容結構 1C，不需製造電晶體結構 1T) ，產能擴充不
排擠 16nm DRAM 之設備與產能配置 。 相較於生產需要耗時 170 天的 DRAM，
矽電容的生產 cycle time 只需要 30 天。在相同的生產時間內， DRAM 跑 1
個生產 cycle ，矽電容則可以跑 5 個 cycle ，這大幅提升設備的周轉率。  
華邦電原本預期矽電容在 2027 年時能占 DRAM 營收的 10% 。然而由於
DRAM 本身的營收分母在近期成長得太大，要達到 10% 的占比變得困難，
目前評估能維持 5%營收占比。不過，矽電容發展的絕對總金額與公司原先
計畫的預期差不多，公司依舊看好其長期的成長動能。 5,400nF/mm² 產品毛
利率接近公司平均水平，  
華邦電宣布將以全現金方式取得 Infineon 的 NOR Flash 與 F-RAM 事業
100% 的股權：  
09/16/2026 華邦電發布重訊，宣布將與德國半導體大廠 Infineon(Infineon) 旗
下的 Infineon Technologies LLC 簽署股份購買協議。華邦電將以全現金約
11.2 億美元 (以匯率 31.6 估算約新台幣 353.92 億元 )，取得 Infineon 
Technologies LLC 旗下 NOR Flash 與 F-RAM 事業經重組後標的公司的 100%
股權。  
交易完成後，華邦電將直接或間接持有標的公司 100% 股權，標的公司並將
重新以「 Spansion 」為名營運 ， 形成Winbond 與 Spansion 雙品牌布局 。Spansion
總部續留美國加州聖荷西 (San Jose) ，保留原 Infineon/Cypress 的研發核心與
歐美技術團隊 (跨十餘國人才 )。

### Page 6

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 4 日

6 群益投顧 
交易總金額將依 Stock Purchase Agreement 所約定之交割日財務狀況及價格
調整機制結算後確定，資金來源為自有資金或融資活動支應。本交易預計於
2H27 完成，惟仍須取得相關主管機關核准，並達成或依法豁免股份購買協
議所約定之其他交割先決條件。  
此次收購對華邦電而言，則可藉此取得完整 NOR Flash 線，並新增 F-RAM
產品與相關全球客戶 、 研發資源 ， 進一步提高在NOR Flash 市 占 率。依Omdia
統計， 2025 年全球 NOR Flash 市場規模約 34 億美元，其中華邦電市占 23%
居首，其次為兆易創新 (603986 SHA) 及旺宏 (2337 TT) ，各占 17% ， Infineon
占 11% 、排名第四。  
從產品及客戶結構來看，雙方既有客戶重疊程度不高。華邦電 NOR Flash 應
用以 PC、 NB 市場為主， Infineon 相關業務則具有較多歐美級車用客戶，有
助公司收購後直接擴大 NOR Flash 規模，並補足不同區域及終端市場的客
戶布局。  
價格維持高檔，預估華邦電 2026 年和 2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 23.68 元和
35.90 元：  
華邦電在 AI 邊緣運算方面，提供 CUBE(Customized Ultra Bandwidth
Element) 方案，並參加 UCIe 聯盟，華邦電自主開發高頻寬 CUBE 記憶體，
適用於邊緣 AI 應用， AI、HPC 領域的  3D TSV DRAM 。目前推進進度符合
預期，主要應用於 AI 加速器 (ASIC/XPU) 之推論運算，公司進行 Wafer-on-
Wafer 堆疊四層 DRAM 並整合客戶單層 SoC ，目前內部三層堆疊成功率超
過 99% ，預計於 2H27 開始小量量產。  
CMS 技術發展與產品組合方面 ， 產品線主要聚焦於 DDR4 與 LPDDR4 。20nm
為當前營收大宗，佔 CMS 事業比重約 66% ，公司同時保留部分 20nm 產能
以支應車用、工業及基礎架構 (Infrastructure) 等客戶對 DDR3 的長期需求。
16nm 已開始投片出貨， 3Q26 佔 CMS 比重為 3%。隨著高雄廠 Module A 擴
產至 24K 至 25K/ 月， 16nm 月產能由 2K 提升至 16K ，公司規劃 2027 年下
半年 16nm 佔記憶體營收超過 40% 以擴大位元產出。客製化超高頻寬記憶體
CUBE 專為 AI 加速器 (Inference 、 XPU 、 ASIC) 設計，晶圓堆疊 (Wafer-on-
Wafer) 試疊三層成功率達 99%，預計 2027 年進入量產並提供 SoC 搭配四層
DRAM 之 3D 堆疊架構 。 此外 ， 公司切入企業級SSD(eSSD) 內建 DRAM Cache
供應鏈，規劃 2027 年起於電腦領域擴大貢獻。  
Flash 技術發展與產品組合方面，公司在 512Mb 、 1Gb 及 2Gb 等高容量領域
建立市場地位， 3Q26 高容量 NOR Flash 營收與出貨佔比均創歷史新高，主
力製程包含 58nm 。SLC NAND 專注於程式碼儲存 (Code Storage) 市場， 24nm
出貨比重持續提升 (3Q26 佔 Flash 營收 7%) 並逐步取代 32nm。 產品組合涵蓋
1.2V 、1.8V 、 3V QSPI/Octal NOR 、 Secure NOR 及 SLC NAND/MCP 等方案。
Flash 市場與營運展望方面， NOR Flash 方面， AI 伺服器架構於
Compute/Switch/Network Tray 、 SmartNIC 、 DPU 、 BMC 及 BIOS/UEFI 等區
塊均搭載 NOR Flash ，伺服器應用佔比提升，以 58nm 製程為大宗。市場上
大於等於 512Mb 的高容量 NOR Flash 供給持續吃緊，公司產能往 512Mb 以
上配置，市佔率超過 60% 至 70%；中國廠商產能主要集中在小於等於 32Mb
或 64Mb 的低容量消費性市場 。 單架新世代 AI 伺服器機櫃需搭載 500 至 600

7 群益投顧 
顆 NOR Flash 。華邦內部預估全球伺服器 NOR Flash 需求量於 2027 年達 2.4
億顆 (2025 年至 2028 年年複合成長率為 33%)，其中大於等於 512Mb 高容量
產品將佔 2027 年總需求之 65%。華邦在 AI 生態系之伺服器 NOR 位元出貨
量預估 2026 年 YoY+112% 、2027 年 YoY+67% 。高容量 NOR 價格上行趨勢
預估延續至 2027 年。 SLC NAND 方面，在 2027 年維持正向價格展望。英
飛凌 NOR Flash 與 F-RAM 事業收購案預計於 2H27 完成交割，後續將以
Spansion 品牌維持獨立營運，以深化高階、車用及國防領域布局。  
華邦電表示， 4Q26 市場需求呈現結構性分化。 PC 及傳統消費性電子進入季
節性休養與庫存修正，需求呈現鬆軟態勢； AI 相關產品受 AI 伺服器、 Edge 
AI 及網通基礎建設拉貨帶動，需求力道維持強勁。華邦電藉由 4Q26 非 AI
需求小幅修整，調控部分出貨與產能，保留產能予簽署長期合約 (LTA) 的策
略夥伴使用。  
在價格與合約價走勢方面，客製化記憶體事業 (CMS/DRAM) 於 4Q26 合約價
格持續微幅向上調漲，整體價格維持走揚。快閃記憶體事業 (Flash) 在 AI 伺
服器與車用需求支撐下，高容量 NOR Flash 供給維持緊缺，價格上行趨勢延
續； SLC NAND 受強勁 Design-ins 支撐，價格展望正面。  
產業供需結構方面，全球主要記憶體同業缺乏大幅新增產能，記憶體供不應
求的緊俏態勢延伸至 2027 年至 2028 年。 2027 年記憶體市況評估將較 2026
年更為吃緊 。 預估華邦電2026 年和 2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 23.68 元和 35.90
元。

  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 3

ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Stable traditional businesses and EMS outlook — Traditional packaging and test
demand is improving, supported by utilization gains and a recovery in automotive
and industrial, as well as AI-adjacent chips. Management expects general-market
business to grow again in 2027, despite softer smartphone and PC demand. For
2027, ASEH expects further EMS growth, led by high-performance computing, and

is pursuing system-level solutions that combine EMS capabilities with ATM
technologies.
LEAP upside intact; GM upward trend structural
Following the solid 3Q26 result, it expects 4Q26 consolidated revenue to grow 6–
8% QoQ, with gross margin of 23–24% and operating margin of 14.5–15.5%. ATM
revenue is forecast to increase 11–13% QoQ, with GM improving 100–200 bps. EMS
revenue is expected to be flat QoQ, with an operating margin of about 2%.
Looking into 2027, management expects LEAP revenue to double, with further GM

improvement as LEAP ramps and utilization rises across traditional packaging and
test. Demand remains ahead of available capacity: assembly is expected to grow
faster than test, with investment prioritizing assembly and wafer probing. Full-
process revenue is projected to exceed US$1bn in 2027, while CPO is expected to
contribute more than US$500m. Capacity and facility space remain key
constraints; ASE plans to increase capex to US$11.5bn in 2027 given its raised

2026 outlook. It also expects traditional-market growth to continue, supported by
automotive, industrial and AI-adjacent demand, despite softer smartphone and PC
markets.
On the back of its structural GM upside and more LEAP revenue contribution, we
lift our 2026/2027 /2028 earnings projection by 12%/18%/16%. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$908, based on an unchanged 25x 2027 EPS.

### Page 8

ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
8
ASE Technology Holding
Company description
Advanced Semiconductor Engineering (ASE), incorporated in 1984 and listed
at the TWSE in 1989, is one of the world's leading companies in the
semiconductor packaging and testing sector. In June 2016, ASE entered into a
Joint Share Exchange Agreement with SPIL under which ASE Industrial was
eventually to be formed as a holding company with 100% equity interests in

both ASE and SPIL. ASE Industrial was listed on Taiwan's main board on April
30, 2018, the same day on which ASE and SPIL were delisted.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on ASE Holding. The combined ASE-SPIL entity is the
highest-quality OSAT worldwide, in our view, with its market share
leadership, superior operations, economies of scale, and customer/product
diversification. Conditions imposed by the Chinese government on the ASE-
SPIL merger, which required them to operate independently, were lifted in

2020. Consequently, we believe ASE Holding will benefit from further revenue
and operating synergies as well as the trend for advanced packaging.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$908 is based on 25x 2027 EPS average. Our PER is
higher than its five-year range as we expect ASEH to see accelerating
earnings growth in the coming years with GM at upward expansion trend,
mainly driven by its promising outlook in LEAP business and strong expansion
for long-term AI demand. At our target price, the shares would trade at a

2026/27E P/B of 10x/8x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent ASE shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) poor efficiency and less synergy created from merger with
SPIL; 2) failure to gain market share in advanced packaging; 3) industry
oversupply due to aggressive capacity expansion; 4) severe pricing erosion
due to competition; and 5) a longer-than-expected semiconductor inventory
correction.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

### Page 3

Mediatek (2454.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Structural OPM upward trend — Management expects 4Q26 to mark the 
beginning of a structural improvement in OPM, supported by rapidly scaling data 
center ASIC revenue and operating leverage. Management indicated that the OPM 
could exceed 20% in 2027. Meanwhile, selective pricing adjustments across 
traditional businesses should help offset rising supply chain costs. Beyond data 
centers, Smart Edge should benefit from new AI PCs, AI tablets, automotive 
platforms and networking applications, including continued collaboration with 
NVIDIA on next-generation AI computing products. While smartphone demand 
remains relatively subdued, management expects the expanding AI business to 
increasingly dominate MediaTek’s overall growth and earnings trajectory.
Second-Generation ASIC on Track for 1Q28 
Production, with a Second Customer 
Management expressed increasing confidence in achieving the upper end of its 15–
20% market share target for the estimated US$80bn data center AI accelerator 
ASIC market in 2027, implying approximately US$16bn in potential revenue. This 
represents a substantial increase from the revised US$2.5bn target for 2026, 
supported by strong demand visibility and continued supply chain expansion. 
Management emphasized that securing adequate foundry, packaging and other 
critical component capacity remains essential to meeting customer requirements. 
Importantly, the revenue opportunity reflects MediaT ek’s increasing participation in 
the value chain, encompassing advanced process technologies, packaging, high-
speed interconnects and custom silicon integration. Despite recent market 
concerns over AI infrastructure financing and CSP capital expenditure 
sustainability, management remains confident that AI compute demand will 
continue growing over the next several years, particularly for customized 
accelerators offering superior performance per watt and total cost of ownership.
Figure 1. Mediatek - 3Q26 results overview 
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Citi Research, Bloomberg
Management reiterated that its second AI accelerator project remains on schedule 
for high-volume production in 1Q28, despite market concerns regarding design 
complexity, advanced packaging yields and potential delays. The new accelerator is 
expected to deliver multiple times the performance per watt of the first-generation 
product, significantly improving customer TCO and strengthening Media T ek’s 
competitive positioning . Management acknowledged manufacturing challenges 
associated with its U.S. IDM packaging partner but highlighted progress in 
identifying key yield constraints and implementing corrective measures, expressing 
high confidence in further yield improvements. Importantly, Media T ek also 
confirmed a separate design win with a second, non-Chinese data center

Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
4
customer, with initial revenue expected in 2028. The company is already
collaborating with its existing customer on subsequent-generation products,
although specifications are still being finalized. Management expects another year
of strong revenue growth in 2028 but declined to provide specific revenue guidance
or customer contribution estimates.
Expanding competitive advantages through advanced node

design/packaging, SerDes and NVIDIA NVLink Fusion
MediaT ek continues investing in advanced technology capabilities, including 448G
SerDes, advanced packaging, HBM integration and co-packaged optics (CPO), to
establish a competitive advantage in increasingly complex AI infrastructure
designs. Management emphasized that its differentiation comes not from
individual technologies but from integrating them into high-performance, power-
efficient and commercially viable solutions. More importantly, its partnership with

NVIDIA through NVLink Fusion enables customers to integrate custom XPUs into
NVIDIA’s rack-scale ecosystem, while its support for UALink and Ethernet-based
architectures provides greater deployment flexibility. MediaT ek is expanding
beyond individual silicon components toward subsystems and rack-level
solutions, shortening customers’ development cycles and accelerating time to
revenue. Management indicated encouraging engagement with multiple potential

customers and expects broader adoption opportunities. The 448G SerDes
development is progressing well and is expected to support both existing
accelerator programs and future customers, with management not anticipating it
to become a critical schedule bottleneck.
Structural OPM expansion trend; Reiterate Buy
Management expects 4Q26 to mark the beginning of a structural improvement in
operating profitability, supported by rapidly scaling data center ASIC revenue and

operating leverage. Although ASIC gross margins are expected to remain below
MediaT ek’s traditional corporate average, the significantly larger revenue
contribution and more favorable operating expense leverage should drive
meaningful operating margin expansion. Management indicated that the operating
margin could exceed 20% in 2027, potentially somewhat higher, compared with its
historical mid-teen level. Meanwhile, selective pricing adjustments across

traditional businesses should help offset rising supply chain costs, although these
increases are primarily intended to preserve profitability rather than expand gross
margins. Beyond data centers, Smart Edge should benefit from new AI PCs, AI
tablets, automotive platforms and networking applications, including continued
collaboration with NVIDIA on next-generation AI computing products. While
smartphone demand remains relatively subdued, management expects the

expanding AI business to increasingly dominate MediaT ek’s overall growth and
earnings trajectory.
Given MediaT ek’s better operating leverage, we lift our earnings projection for
2027 /2028 5%/5%, mainly reflecting better OPM. We maintain our Buy rating with
an unchanged TP of NT$6,800, implying 29x our 2027 /28 EPS estimates.

### Page 9

Mediatek (2454.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
9
Mediatek
Company description
Mediatek (MTK) is a fabless IC company that designs SoC for smartphone,
smart TV, and digital media devices. It also develops chips for connectivity,
networking, smart home gadgets, and automotive sectors. It was founded in
1997, is listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange, and is headquartered in Taiwan.
It has sales and research subsidiaries in mainland China, Singapore, India,

the US, Japan, Korea, Denmark, and England. It primarily sells its products in
China and other emerging-market countries.
Investment strategy
We rate Mediatek shares as Buy. We expect Mediatek to ride on 5G migration
in emerging markets and market share gain of its high-end Dimensity series in
China market as well. In particular, we see strong growth potential from its AI
ASIC, NTN-satellite business in the long term. We believe solid fundamentals

and strong operating leverage will support Mediatek’s margins outlook.
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for Mediatek is based on 29x P/E to
2027 /2028 EPS, which is 1-std above its five-year average forward P/E given
the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We
believe our target PE is also justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers'
trading at an average of c32x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should

provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek as well
prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication. Despite lukewarm
smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G
migration in emerging markets and its Dimensity series should also gain
shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing
stabilizing TV and Io T demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market
on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.

Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from AI ASIC business; 2)
more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall
demand related to macroeconomic risks, especially for smartphone demand;
and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Earnings revision:  
We revise up our 2026 earnings estimates by 1% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
down our 2026E revenue estimates by 18%). We revise up 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 14%/23% to reflect a better product mix with higher contribution from the server 
business (we revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 2/4%). We now expect the 
GM expansion to be faster due to the higher server business contribution, and thus 
revise up GPM by 5.3/3.1/4.4ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Valuation:  
We reiterate our Buy rating on Fositek with a new 12m TP of NT$2,390 (from NT$2,082), 
based on 22.6 2027E P/E (unchanged), which is the company’s average P/E since 2022, 
and factoring in our earnings revision.

### Page 3

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Fositek is a global leading hinges supplier, mainly for foldable smartphones (56% of 
2025 revenues). We expect the company’s foldable smartphone hinges revenues to 
show solid growth in 2026/27E driven by (1) rising foldable phone market penetration 
rate; (2) expanding customer base, from China smartphones to more global-tier 
smartphones; and (3) stabilized smartphone market demand, encouraging brand makers 
to launch new form factors to attract consumers. Competitive edges of Fositek lie in its 
strong innovation (e.g., hinges for rollable phones, wearable phones), in-house molding 
and automated production to secure precision, long experience to support fast delivery, 
and leading customer base with expansion (e.g., supplying Huawei since the first 
foldable phone back in 2019).  We continue to see Fositek successfully entering the 
AI-related business, especially QD (quick disconnect) products for AI server liquid 
cooling. The company has the largest QD production capacity (4.5mn units/month) 
among Taiwanese thermal suppliers and will continue to expand if demand continues to 
accelerate. Furthermore, the company continues to make progress with its rail kit 
business, which will continue to break into AI servers (including both GPU servers and 
ASIC servers). Overall, we believe Fositek will successfully transition from a 
consumer-focused company to AI-focused company, with server-related business to 
contribute 94% of total revenue in 2028E (vs. 50% in 2025), and will see GM continue to 
expand to 37.2% in 2028E (vs. 26.3% in 2025).  
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Fositek 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$2,390 is based on a target 22.6x 2027 P/E, 
which is the company’s average P/E since 2022. We consider this multiple appropriate 
given the increasing contribution from AI-related products (including QD and rail kits) 
could offset the potential weakness from its foldable smartphone business. 
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected contribution from AI-related products, 
(2) slower-than-expected foldable phone penetration; and (3) Potential competition 
from more suppliers

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預

個股報告 
富世達(6805) 
伺服器占比突破八成，QD量價齊揚推升獲利 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $3000 
前次投資建議 2026.08.05: 
「買進」 ，目標價: $2360

報告日期：2026.11.04 研 究 員：陳威仲

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 26Q3 毛利率優於預期，伺服器產品營收占比升至 81.3%，上修 2026/27 年 EPS 至
65.5/120.1 元(前次預估 64.3/112.8元)， 目標價由2360 元調升至 3000 元(25X 27(F)PER)，
維持【買進】投資評等。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/3 收盤價： 2160.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 45.0 億元 (QoQ+20.4%/YoY+26.2%)，受
VR200、ASIC 平台小量拉貨與非奇鋐之快接頭客戶出貨
增加帶動；毛利率 38.0%(季增 3.9ppts/年增 10.3ppts，
優於預期 2.9ppts)，主因伺服器產品營收占比升至
81.3%；營業淨利 14.6 億元(QoQ+39.9%/YoY+84.2%)；
稅後淨利 12.4億元(QoQ+46.3%/YoY+80.8%)，EPS 18.1
元。 
➢ QD 受惠 GPU 與 ASIC 雙平台放量及規格升級，Rubin 
Ultra 將導入單價約兩倍的 ZQD，AWS Trn3 等 ASIC 專
案將於 2027 年陸續貢獻；AI 滑軌亦陸續通過驗證，27
年可望隨 VR200與 ASIC專案放量。 
➢ 華為摺疊機新品拉貨遞延，加上記憶體漲價影響終端需
求 ， 下 修26Q4 營 收 預 估 至54.0 億元
(QoQ+20.0%/YoY+35.8%)；惟伺服器產品比重上升，毛
利率上修至 38.1%，EPS 21.63元。 
➢ 預估 2026/27 年 EPS 65.5/120.1 元，將持續受惠強勁的
伺服器需求與水冷滲透率提升之下帶動獲利成長，目前股
價分別位於 2026/27 年 EPS的 33.0/18.0X，過去股價區
間落在 15~87X，目標價 3000 元(25X 27(F)PER)，維持
【買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空間 38.9%。

營 運 分 析

富世達為 AI基礎設施與高階摺疊機轉軸的關鍵供應商 
富世達股份有限公司成立於民國 2001 年，為奇鋐(3017)持股 16.87%的轉投
資公司，總部與研發中心位於新北新莊，並於越南、中國深圳設有生產基地，
提供各類金屬沖壓設計與製造，主要產品為伺服器組件(滑軌、水冷快接頭)，
與摺疊手機、筆記型電腦、平板電腦之轉軸零件，從消費性電子零組件廠轉
型為 AI基礎設施與高階摺疊機轉軸的關鍵供應商，主要出貨終端包含華為、
Motorola 等摺疊機，與各大AI伺服器平台(NVIDIA、AMD、ASIC等)機櫃用
的滑軌與水冷快接頭。

26Q3 伺服器產品營收占比上升至 81.3%，帶動毛利率大幅優於預期。 
富世達 26Q3 營收 45.0 億元(QoQ+20.4%/YoY+26.2%，優於兆豐前次預期
4%)，因 VR200、ASIC 平台小量拉貨與非奇鋐之快接頭客戶規模上升使伺
服器快接頭營收增長強勁；毛利率 38.0%(季增 3.9ppts/年增 10.3ppts，優於
兆豐預期 2.9ppts)，主因產品組合改善，伺服器產品營收占比超越 80%；營
業淨利 14.6 億元 (QoQ+39.9%/YoY+84.2%)， 稅 後 淨 利12.4 億元
(QoQ+46.3%/YoY+80.8%)，EPS 18.1元(前次預估 15.9 元)。

資料來源：Cmoney、兆豐投顧整理與預估

QD 受惠 GPU與 ASIC雙平台放量及規格升級，為伺服器產品主要成長動能 
26Q3 富世達伺服器產品占營收 81.3%，其中快接頭(QD)約 90%，整體單季
稼動率約 80~90%；而目前新產能開出後 QD 產能已達 1350 萬顆/季。今年
QD 營收貢獻以 GPU 平台為主，明年 ASIC 比重將持續提高，預期 2028 年
ASIC 比重有望超越 GPU；其中(1)AWS Trn3 已配合客戶時程出貨，將於
27H1顯著貢獻營收。(2)Google TPU V9世代有望由現行的 NSP1接頭，變
為導入混用 QD+NSP1接頭，以優化未來光模塊、DIMM 等部件潛在導入水
冷系統後維護的容易性。(3)Meta MTIA 與微軟 MAIA 等 AISC 專案也將於
27H2 出貨帶動 QD 市場規模提升。而在 Rubin Ultra 世代，將導入 ZQD 於
運算托盤(取代部份的 UQD)與 rack manifold之快接頭，且ZQD單顆價值預
估將為現行 MQD與 UQD 單價的兩倍，ZQD 預計 12月進入量產準備。 
規格面，VR200 單櫃 QD 數量約 538 顆(含 rack manifold 88 顆)，Rubin 
Ultra(VR300)將為 574~664顆，預估 QD量價齊揚趨勢延續，為2027~2028
年主要成長動能。

AI 滑軌陸續通過驗證，27年可望隨 VR200與 ASIC 專案量產放量 
滑軌事業目前月產能維持 35 萬套，目前以通用型為主，稼動率約 3 成；隨
明年 AI專案的量產，稼動率可望提升至50~60%以上，毛利率也將隨著 ASIC
水冷專案的放量同步改善，水冷櫃滑軌對承重、緩衝與平行度要求較高，ASP
亦較高，公司預期明年滑軌維持高度成長，後續將視訂單規劃擴廠。

- 4 - 
個股報告

華為摺疊機新品拉貨遞延與記憶體推升價格，轉軸營收短期承壓 
華為新機種受記憶體影響拉貨延後，加上終端需求轉弱，26Q3富世達手機
軸承營收占比降至 15.5%，預期26Q4手機轉軸拉貨動能有限。而華為 26H1
中國摺疊機市占逾 7成，摺疊機累計出貨突破 2,000 萬支；惟記憶體漲價使
單機平均成本增加約 200 美元，9/1 起主力機型最高調漲 17%，後續產品將
朝輕薄化發展並導入新材料，每代價格將重新議價，下一代機種仍由富世達
主供。雖蘋果 iPhone Duo 上市帶動摺疊機話題，將使摺疊機長期接受度與
滲透率同步提高，但短期消費性電子仍受記憶體價格推升單價而影響終端購
買意願。

26Q4 營收下修、毛利率上修，獲利預估小幅調降 
預估富世達 26Q4 營收 54.0 億元(QoQ+20.0%/YoY+35.8%，較原估下修
8.2%，主因摺疊機軸承拉貨不如預期 )；毛利率 38.1%(季增 0.1ppts/年增
9.0ppts，較原估上修 1.1ppts，主因 QD 生產進入規模化，帶動高毛利伺服
器比重上升)；稅後淨利 14.8 億元(QoQ+19.4%/YoY+97.3%，較原估下修
4.7%)，EPS 21.63元(原估 22.7 元)。

## Document 275: AI忧虑冲击科技股，老虎环球、Lone Pine等选股基金10月惨遭重创

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI忧虑冲击科技股，老虎环球、Lone Pine等选股基金10月惨遭重创.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

受 AI 投资前景担忧及美国中期选举冲击，多家头部选股型对冲基⾦损失惨重： Lone Pine 亏
损约 9% 、年内转负；⽼虎环球跌 8.5% 、涨幅归零； Atreides 旗下对冲基⾦暴跌 16% 。
Coatue 逆势上涨 0.2% 成为少数亮点。多策略及量化基⾦跌幅相对温和。
摘要：
围绕⼈⼯智能投资的担忧引发科技股⼤规模抛售，多家头部选股型对冲基⾦在 10 ⽉遭受两位数
级别的损失，部分基⾦年内涨幅⼏近悉数回吐。
11 ⽉ 6 ⽇据彭博社援引知情⼈⼠透露， Steve Mandel 麾下Lone Pine Capital上⽉亏损约 9% ，此
前该基⾦已将年内⾼达 44% 的累计涨幅悉数抹去，⽬前 2026 年全年录得约 7% 的亏损，在同类基
⾦中表现居于末位。
⽼虎环球管理旗下对冲基⾦10 ⽉下跌 8.5% ，年内涨幅⼏乎归零；Whale Rock 资管公司旗舰基
⾦下挫约 8% ， 2026 年涨幅收窄⾄约 34% 。
与此同时，⺠主党在美国中期选举中取得压倒性胜利后，以科技股为主的纳斯达克综合指数周

四单⽇下跌 5.5% ，创 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⼤跌幅，显⽰市场抛压仍在延续。
这场⻛波席卷多种对冲基⾦策略，芯⽚制造商股价受冲击尤为惨烈。中期选举实际上演变为市
场对新数据中⼼建设前景的⼀次公投， AI 融资忧虑由此转化为对科技板块的集中抛售。
Lone Pine 损失最重，年内收益全部蒸发
在此轮调整中， Lone Pine Capital 的处境最为艰难。
据报道，该基⾦ 10 ⽉亏损约 9% ，⽽在此之前，其年内涨幅已从⾼点的 44% 逐步被侵蚀殆尽，⽬
前 2026 年全年下跌约 7% ，在同类选股基⾦中跌幅居前。
相⽐之下，⽼虎环球旗下对冲基⾦10 ⽉下跌 8.5% 后，年内涨幅已基本归零。其规模较⼩的交叉
基⾦跌幅相对温和，上⽉下跌 3.3% ，年内仍保有 15.3% 的涨幅。
Whale Rock 旗舰公私混合基⾦下跌约 8% 后， 2026 年累计涨幅收窄⾄约 34% ；该公司旗下纯股
票多头基⾦跌幅更深， 10 ⽉重挫 12.3% ，年内涨幅收窄⾄ 30.6% 。

Dan Sundheim 麾下D1 Capital Partners 的股票投资组合上⽉下跌 4.4% ，年内回报率由此降⾄
18.8% 。
Atreides Management 旗下资产管理规模约 34 亿美元的旗舰公私混合基⾦10 ⽉下跌 9.5% ，降幅
在同类基⾦中位居前列。受益于对 SpaceX 及多家⾮上市公司的投资，该基⾦今年迄今仍录得约
43% 的涨幅。
Atreides旗下规模较⼩的对冲基⾦处境则更为窘迫 —— 上⽉重挫 16% ，年内涨幅⼤幅收窄⾄仅
剩 2.5% 。
Coatue 独善其⾝，多策略基⾦分化加剧
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

在选股基⾦普遍受挫的背景下， Philippe Laffont 的 Coatue Management 是为数不多的例外。其
对冲基⾦ 10 ⽉上涨 0.2% ，今年迄今累计上涨 13% ，在同类基⾦中表现相对突出。
从更⼴泛的对冲基⾦⾏业来看，各类策略之间的分化在 10 ⽉明显加剧。
多策略基⾦同样难以完全幸免： Balyasny 下跌 3.4% ， Millennium 下跌 2.3% ， ExodusPoint 下跌
2.4% ；⽽ Citadel Wellington 上涨 1.7% ， D.E. Shaw Composite 上涨 0.7% ，表现相对稳健。
量化基⾦与宏观基⾦整体跌幅较选股类基⾦更为温和， CFM Discus 、 Winton Diversified Macro
等⼩幅收涨，但 Marshall Wace TOPS 下跌 3.6% ， Kirkoswald 跌幅则⾼达 6.4% 。

Key points
• The Cotality home value index – covering
all of Australia – fell 1.2% in October
following a 1.3% decline in September
and 1.4% falls in August and July.

Annual growth has turned negative,
running at –2.8%yr with prices down
6.7% from their peak in March.
• Across the major cities, Brisbane and
Perth led the declines in the October
month, recording falls of 2.0% and
1.7% respectively, but the correction
remains more pronounced in Sydney
(prices down a further 1.6%mth to be
off 9.4%yr) with Melbourne also off
more sharply over the year (–7.6%yr)
but recording a milder October
(–0.8%mth). Adelaide remains relatively

resilient, recording a 1.2% decline in
the month but prices still up 3.7%yr.
• Preliminary estimates show the
number of sales, in rolling 3mth
terms, down 5.6%qtr and 22.5%yr.

### Page 2

forecasts are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The Cotality home value index, covering all of Australia, fell
1.2% in October with slight revisions to previous months
again shaving a further 0.3% off prices. The correction
has now seen prices decline 6.7% from their March peak

with annual growth firmly in negative at –2.8%yr. Turnover
continues fall as well, down 22.5%yr. Housing-related
tax policy changes, interest rate rises and heightened
uncertainty have triggered a sharp contraction in markets.
The downturn is now comparable to the previous two
housing market corrections. Nationally, the downturn in
2022-23 saw prices decline 7.5% and turnover fall 29% peak
to trough. The 2017-18 correction saw prices fall 8.6% and

turnover by 24%, with larger falls in Sydney and Melbourne.
Note that unless specified, figures in titles, tables and
commentary are in non‑seasonally adjusted terms, consistent
with the ‘headline’ figures reported by Cotality. However,
figures shown in charts are in seasonally adjusted terms.
Preliminary estimates of turnover show rolling 3mth volumes
down 5.6%qtr in October, previous estimates again revised
lower. The monthly pattern suggests declines re ‑accelerated

through September‑October after stabilising somewhat in July ‑
August. The RBA's interest rate rise in late September and a
further expected tightening likely contributed to the shift.
The detail shows Brisbane and Perth are now leading the
way in terms of monthly price declines although corrections
to date remain significantly larger in Sydney and Melbourne
while smaller capitals and regional areas continue to see much
milder downturns. Across price tiers, all segments are now

showing similar rates of decline after top price tiers had seen
a more pronounced and earlier downturn. 'Top tier' segments
have tended to lead recent price cycles.
Looking across jurisdictions,
• Sydney prices dropped 1.6% in October having averaged
similar‑sized declines over the previous five months. While
all segments and tiers continue to record falls, the last few
months has seen the pace of decline moderate a touch in the
top tier segments that have led cycles in the past, although

they are still a long way from stabilisation.
• Melbourne prices declined 0.8%mth, in line with
September's move and continuing the slower pace of
retreat. As noted, this likely reflects the weaker starting
point coming into the correction, prices already at 5½ year
lows. Interestingly, the detail shows the moderation in price
declines in recent months has been more pronounced for
'top tier' units where prices are back where they were in
2016.
Housing downturn deepens

### Page 3

forecasts are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially
from these forecasts.
3WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
• Brisbane prices are tracking a very fast rate of decline
that is also seeing large downward revisions. Prices fall
2% in October with September and August now both
showing 2.1% falls, revised down from 1.5% and 1.6%

respectively (August having previously been estimated at
just 1%, July's move having seen a similar series of revisions
–1.2% to –1.8%). The combined effect has taken the rolling
3mth change to –6.1%qtr from –4.7%qtr last months and
–2.7%qtr in August and –0.6%qtr in July. At 0.4%yr, annual
price growth has virtually stalled flat having run at 18%yr as
recently as May. All tiers and segments are recording similar
rates of decline.
• Perth is seeing something very similar, prices dropping a

further 1.7% in October, with September's decline revised
down to 2.1% from –1.2% and August's to 2.0% from –1.7%.
At 3.9%yr, annual growth is still positive but off steeply
from the 24% pace back in May. As with Brisbane, all tiers
and segments are recording similar rates of decline.
• Adelaide dwelling prices posted a somewhat milder 1.2%
decline in October, , September's –1.3% decline marked
down to –1.7%. Annual price growth slowed to 3.7%yr,

similar to Perth's but with less of a whiplash from strong
gains to large declines. Turnover has also been much
steadier, sales holding flat over the last three months and
down just 12.4%yr.
• The smaller capitals were again more mixed, price growth
still positive in Darwin (0.6%mth, 9.7%yr), relatively resilient
in Hobart (+0.5%mth, 5.5%yr) but weaker in Canberra
(–0.7%mth, –3.7%yr).
• Regional markets showed a 0.9% decline in Qld but slightly
milder declines in NSW (–0.7%) and Victoria (0.6%). Annual

prices growth is in the 1.4 ‑3.5%yr range.
All up, the October updates make for bleak reading. With the
RBA widely expected to raise interest rates again, the near
term outlook remains very challenging. Next week's consumer
sentiment update will show how this is shaping housing ‑
related sentiment – so far, consumer price expectations have
weakened but stopped short of swinging to the clear negative
consensus that emerged in the 2022 ‑23 correction. That now

looks to be at risk. Either way, momentum looks set to remain
weak into year‑end and, barring a significantly improved
inflation and interest rate outlook, well into 2027.

## Document 310: Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

Central banks: a pause while waiting for more information

As expected, the Fed, the ECB, and the BoJ left their rates unchanged this week. This pause , however, does not mark 
the end of the cycle. The ECB’s tone is becoming more balanced, weighing inflationary risks against tightening financial 
conditions, but one final rate hike in December remains our base case. Across the Atlantic, Kevin Warsh describes an 
environment conducive to further tightening, while remaining cautious about the risk of a credit shock. In Japan, the 
lasting exit from the deflationary cycle is taking shape, and monetary policy adjustment is expected to continue 
gradually. We anticipate two rate hikes by the Fed by January and four by the BoJ by the second half of 2028.

Eurozone: A more balanced tone, but the course remains unchanged. The ECB’s statement marks a shift from September. 
It now explicitly acknowledges both upside risks to inflation (the magnitude and persistence of the energy shock) and downsid e 
pressures (tightening financial conditions). The statement that inflation would  remain “well above target for an extended period” 
no longer appears. In essence, however, there is little change. A rate hike in December remains our base case scenario, widel y 
anticipated and implicitly signaled by Christine Lagarde during the Q&A (“If the current situation is not called into question by the 
next set of economic data, the path is clear”). Lagarde presented the rise in sovereign yields as a global phenomenon that is  
tightening financial conditions; regarding the TPI, she stuck to the usual stance: the ECB will intervene if monetary policy 
transmission were to be hampered “by disorderly movements not justified by fundamentals” . By December, the indirect and 
second-round effects of the energy shock will be closely scrutini sed. From our perspective, September’s producer prices offer 
clearer signals, but the increase remains limited to a few sectors: energy, electricity-intensive industries and electrical equipment, 
driven by IA 1. For now, companies in the Eurozone, particularly in the services sector, appear to be absorbing the shock. The 
European Commission’s survey released this Thursday indicates a rise in the sales price index in October, which remains in line 
with recent months.

United States: The Fed navigates between risks. The FOMC pauses after September’s rate hike and maintains the federal 
funds target at 3.75%-4.0%. However, Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh outlined a landscape conducive to further tightening: stronger 
growth, “rather positive” labor market conditions, and, above all, inflation that is “too high.” As a result, two dissenting votes (out 
of 12) were cast in favor of a 25bps hike.  But the committee also appears to fear the adverse consequences of tightening too 
quickly, which is why it is waiting to “accumulate more information.” AI is increasingly at the heart of monetary policymaking. In 
particular, K. Warsh warned against the risks of an abrupt tightening of interest rate conditions, including for the AI investment 
cycle - which triggered a steepening of the yield curve. He also announced that he would soon (on November 16) attend a hearing 
of the “Productivity and Employment” task force to “refine his views” on the implications of AI for the dual mandate. Governor Lisa 
Cook, who voted for a 25bps hike, had noted ahead of the committee meeting “ongoing inflationary pressures” and a risk that they 
might not “subside quickly”. In addition, K. Warsh announced that there would be fewer meetings resulting in rate decisions: 7 in 
2027 and 6 in 2028, compared with 8 today. The change is expected to take effect starting in the fall of 2027. The meetings freed 
up by this change would allow the committee to focus on “strategic deliberations” and long-term issues.

Japan: A status quo, but a tightening cycle set to continue as the economy emerges from deflation. The Bank of Japan 
decided, in line with our forecasts and the consensus, to keep its policy rate target steady at 1.25% at its October 29-30 meeting. 
One dissenting vote called for a rate hike (25bps), contrasting with the two dovish dissenting votes (opposed to a rate hike) at the 
previous meeting. As in the US, t he decision is justified by the need to further analy se risks to economic activity, prices, and 
financial conditions. Above all, the central bank’s outlook for economic activity and prices marks a turning point: it explicitly states 
that “core inflation has reached a level generally in line with price stability” and that it is “expected to remain there.” Governor Ueda 
refined this by noting that it is now necessary to “anchor” inflation at this level before declaring the target achieved. Furthermore, 
the emphasis on “upside risks to prices” and the fact that an y rise in core inflation is now deemed “undesirable” indicate that 
monetary tightening will continue.

The monetary tightening cycle continues. The next steps are likely to be more significant in the United States, where inflation 
has exceeded the target for nearly six years and coexists with a favorable outlook for growth and employment. In the Eurozone, 
the rise in inflation is primarily due to a supply shock in energy costs (though it is also fueled by demand in the United St ates). It 
continues to have little impact on measures of core inflation, including those tracked by the ECB, such as the H ICP; and 
households’ long-term inflation expectations remain anchored. The degree of monetary tightening also differs. In real terms, the 
ECB’s current rates (the deposit facility rate deflated by the core CPI) hover around 0%, a level that more or less corresponds to 
estimates of the natural rate. The Fed, meanwhile, remains in accommodative territory: the real rate (Fed Funds deflated by Core

1 This is a point underscored by the first PPI releases for September (Spain, Italy).

### Page 2

PCE) is at around 0.8%, 40bps below the committee’s implied long-term level (1.2%). Japan remains an outlier: the rise in rates 
preceded the inflationary shock and, until now, has been more a matter of normalization. It is therefore more extensive and spread 
out over time. The central bank is adjusting to the new inflation regime and remains cautious in its exit from accommodative 
policies. The starting point is significantly lower, with real rates still negative, but the adjustment cycle should ultimately bring it to 
the upper end of the neutral range estimated by the BoJ (1.1% -2.5%). We therefore anticipate one final rate hike by the ECB in 
December, two more hikes by the Fed, first in December and then in January, and four spaced-out hikes by the BoJ through the 
second half of 2028.

Anis Bensaidani   
Economist, United States, Japan 
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

## Document 317: Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-07)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-07).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-07
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

### Page 4

Monthly Economics
Sector Analysis
Personal Consumption Expenditures (PCE)
• We've lifted our Q3 expectation for real PCE on incoming data and a slightly firmer consumer
backdrop in the wake of recent revisions.
Consumer spending looks firmer in the third quarter, and we now expect real PCE to rise at a 3.4%
annualized clip. This upward revision reflects recent data, with August consumption coming in firm
with real spending rising by the fastest monthly clip in nearly two years, but also reflects a slightly

firmer consumer backdrop in the wake of the BEA's annual revisions released late September. Although
revisions did not materially alter spending, they lifted the level of household income in recent years,
which also boosted the personal saving rate. The trajectory of real income growth remains weak, but
the consumer still looks to be in a slightly stronger financial position than previously believed, which
helps explain recent resilience in spending and suggests consumers may have a bit more runway ahead.

Investment: Equipment, Intellectual Property Products and Inventories
• The AI build out will lead to another surge in business fixed investment spending in Q3, though the
effect on headline growth will be partly offset by stronger imports.
Business fixed investment spending continues to be supported primarily by the high-tech buildout.
We forecast real equipment investment to rise at around a 16% annualized clip in Q3, but part of
this boost to headline GDP growth will be offset by stronger import growth that is facilitating the

build out. Net imports of computers are tracking to surge in the third quarter, which will provide a
sizable lift to information processing equipment investment, or around half of overall equipment.
And while non-defense capital goods shipments (including aircraft) slipped in August, the near-term
growth trajectory remains supportive of a pickup in equipment. The rebuild in inventories we have
been anticipating is also tracking to be a bit stronger as businesses replenish drawn down product.

Investment: Residential
• The abrupt rise in mortgage rates is likely to dampen residential investment over the next few
quarters. Residential spending should find support next year as longer-term interest rates
moderate somewhat.
We are unlikely to see another positive residential investment reading in Q3. Rising mortgage rates

have sent a chill over builder outlooks, pushing sales expectations to their weakest level in more than
three years. While builders have responded with deeper price cuts, affordability pressures continue
to weigh on single-family sales and construction outlays. Apartment construction is simultaneously
soft amid rising financing costs and modest rent growth, and home improvement spending remains
constrained by high materials costs and interest rates. While residential investment is likely to remain

4 | Economics

### Page 5

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
a drag this year, we expect a slight moderation in longer-term interest rates in 2027, which would
provide a more supportive backdrop.
Investment: Nonresidential Structures
• We have not made material changes to our nonresidential structures forecast. Despite elevated
interest rates, data center and related spending should continue to support structures investment
through our forecast horizon.
Nonresidential structures spending has accelerated sharply, overwhelmingly driven by data centers

and electric power facilities. As data center construction expands geographically and demand rises
for semiconductors, high-tech capex has also started to generate positive spillovers for warehousing
and manufacturing construction. This buildout, which appears relatively insensitive to interest rates,
should be enough to sustain solid growth in structures investment even as spending across most
other categories remains essentially flat. Domestic energy is another green shoot, with elevated prices

supporting ongoing growth in active rotary rigs.

Net Exports & Trade
• An expected drag from trade in Q3 got larger in light of a near-record trade deficit in August, net
exports is now expected to lop off three full percentage points from third quarter growth.

Exports increased in August for the first time in four months, but the increase was more than offset by
an even larger increase in imports. The upshot was a widening in the trade deficit to a near-record gap
of more than $105 billion. Going into the report, we had a 1.9 percentage point drag from net exports
penciled in for Q3 GDP; we revised that to a full 3.0 percentage points drag in light of the record gap.
Even a modest narrowing in the final months of the year would likely mean a lift to headline GDP in

Q4, but the real story here is that trade will remain a headwind throughout the forecast period owing
largely to surging imports of tech-related products amid the AI boom.
Labor Market
• The jobs market remains stable. We continue to expect the unemployment rate to end the year at
4.2% and hold near that level in 2027.
We modestly revised down our near-term payroll growth forecast following the September jobs
report, but otherwise we have left our labor market outlook unchanged. Measures of labor demand

growth continue to point to a stable labor market. Job openings have inched higher on a year-ago
basis but remain broadly steady while initial jobless claims, WARN notices and Challenger job cut
announcements continue to signal low layoffs. Labor force participation appears to be improving after
its sharp pullback in the first half of the year. Looking ahead, we expect payroll growth to average
around 60K per month in 2027, roughly in line with our estimate of breakeven job growth. That should

Economics | 5

### Page 6

Monthly Economics
keep the unemployment rate near 4.2% in 2027 and leave labor market conditions broadly consistent
with "full employment." Notably, our expectation for labor cost growth of ~3.4% is the reason we
continue to reiterate that labor cost growth is not a contributor to the excess inflation seen in the
data.
Inflation
• We expect the core PCE deflator to rise 3.2% and 2.6% Q4/Q4 in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Headline inflation remains heavily influenced by developments in the Middle East, with higher oil

prices lifting gasoline and diesel prices. Food inflation has been tame this year but looks poised to
strengthen as higher transportation and commodity prices work their way through supply chains.
Beyond food and energy, price pressures remain concentrated in a handful of categories, most notably
airfares and AI-related tech goods. Core goods inflation is showing signs of stabilizing, as waning tariff-
related price pressures have been offset by resilient demand tied to the AI buildout. We expect some

improvement here in 2027. Still-low shelter inflation and cooler price growth for airfares and health
care should help bring about some moderation in core services inflation next year. We expect the core
PCE deflator to rise 3.2% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis in 2026 before slowing to 2.6% in 2027.

Fiscal Policy
• Fiscal year (FY) 2026 ended on September 30, and the federal government tallied a $2.0 trillion
budget deficit (6.5% of GDP). We project the deficit to be unchanged in dollar terms in FY 2027

and a bit smaller as a share of GDP (~6%).
• Another continuing resolution to extend government funding past the December 11 deadline
seems like the most likely outcome at this time for the FY 2027 budget. It is possible Republicans
use the lame duck session of Congress to increase defense spending and raise the debt ceiling.
Looking ahead to the lame duck session of Congress that will follow the midterm elections, Congress
needs to pass a bill that extends government funding past December 11 to avert a government

shutdown. Another continuing resolution that punts the issue into Q1 seems like the most likely
outcome. Perhaps more interesting will be whether Republicans attempt to use budget reconciliation
to pass one more partisan piece of legislation. If they do, we suspect the two things at the top the
agenda will be more funding for national defense and immigration enforcement priorities as well as a
debt ceiling increase. For the latter, raising it in the lame duck would remove some leverage Democrats

could have in 2027 if they recapture one or both chambers. It is still very early, but we estimate the
debt ceiling "X date" by which Congress must act or face default is sometime in late Q3/early Q4 of
next year.
6 | Economics

### Page 7

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
Monetary Policy & Interest Rates
• Our base case forecast is for the FOMC to hike the fed funds rate by 25 bps in December and
then keep the policy rate unchanged in 2027 at 4.00%-4.25%, with risks skewed toward additional
tightening.
As we go to print, the FOMC seems more likely than not to keep the fed funds rate unchanged in
October. But given the rationale under which the Fed started this tightening cycle and the forcefulness

with which Warsh has driven home his displeasure with elevated inflation, it seems entirely plausible
to us that October will be a live meeting. At a minimum, the recent inflation data seem to make a hike
in December a lock. Past December, the outlook will be highly dependent on future inflation data. Our
expectation is that core inflation will cool gradually in 2027, and we still do not see signs of demand-
side inflation in the data. But hiking cycles have an inertial component to them, and the recent inflation

data reset the clock to zero on the Fed seeing several good inflation prints in a row. This creates upside
risk to our base case for the fed funds rate.

International Developments
• Global growth remains resilient, leaving our 2026 and 2027 forecasts unchanged. Our inflation
forecasts are also unchanged, although risks of delayed price pass-through could prolong inflation
pressures into 2027.

• Monetary policy continues to lean toward tightening across advanced economies, while emerging
market central banks are increasingly cautious, either holding rates or easing more gradually as
inflation pressures rebuild.
The balance of risks to global inflation remains tilted to the upside, complicating the monetary policy
outlook even as growth has proven more resilient than expected. Elevated energy prices, particularly
for refined petroleum products, persistent geopolitical uncertainty and El Niño-related food price

risks could prolong inflationary pressures into 2027. While second-round effects still remain relatively
contained, repeated supply shocks are testing central banks’ willingness to look through the temporary
price increases, particularly where inflation expectations remain elevated, even as higher government
bond yields are already contributing to tighter financial conditions.
Across advanced economies, central banks face increasingly difficult trade-offs as persistent inflation

pressures limit the scope for policy accommodation. The Eurozone remains particularly vulnerable
to energy supply disruptions, with elevated natural gas prices and low storage levels adding to
inflation risks. We continue to expect a 25 bps European Central Bank hike in December, followed by a
prolonged hold through 2027. Fiscal concerns are also adding to pressure, particularly in France, where
the selloff in government debt has pushed yields higher. In the U.K., resilient activity and renewed

Economics | 7

### Page 8

Monthly Economics
inflation pressures similarly underpin our expectation for a Bank of England hike in November,
followed by a hold through 2027.
Elsewhere, policy paths are becoming more differentiated. After four hikes this year brought the Cash
Rate to 4.60%, we expect the Reserve Bank of Australia to remain on hold, although renewed inflation
pressures or stronger-than-expected activity could prompt further tightening. We also still expect
the Bank of Canada to hold at 2.25%, with subdued growth weighing against energy-driven inflation

risks, although incoming inflation data over the coming weeks will be critical in determining whether
persistent price pressures warrant tightening monetary policy perhaps as early as the December
meeting. Japan remains an exception to the broader hold outlook: while the yen's recovery provides
some relief from imported inflation, elevated energy prices and import dependence continue to pose
upside risks. We expect the Bank of Japan to hike again by year-end and now expect an additional 25

bps hike in Q3-2027, bringing the terminal rate to 1.75%.
The emerging market story has increasingly shifted from easing toward patience or selective
tightening. In Asia, China's weak domestic demand and persistent property-sector strains continue to
warrant policy support, with AI-related exports providing some offset. We expect a more gradual pace
of Reserve Requirement Ratio cuts alongside targeted monetary and fiscal measures. India faces the

opposite challenge, with elevated food and energy prices and rupee depreciation strengthening the
case for further Reserve Bank of India tightening. In Latin America, peso depreciation and elevated
energy costs pose upside inflation risks for Mexico, although limited exchange-rate pass-through
should allow Banxico to remain on hold while policymakers monitor underlying inflation and inflation
expectations. In Brazil, election-related uncertainty and renewed inflation pressures are likely to delay

further easing, with cuts expected to resume in Q1-2027 once policymakers see more convincing
evidence of disinflation.
